- **analyze-handoff**: Slim same-day session resumption — reads ONLY the project's HANDOFF.md (or top pickup point of CONTEXT.md / continuation/context.md if HANDOFF absent) and produces a 3-line summary (last completed / next intended / blocker) plus the docket (open items by ID, with status markers). Skips memory dir, archive, and wiki body. Costs ~5K tokens vs analyze-context's 50K+. Fires on explicit `/analyze-handoff`, `/handoff`, "quick resume", "where was I", or "what's next" — but ONLY when same-day continuation is clear. Do NOT fire when the user asks for a full briefing ("catch me up", "brief me", "what's… ([bradspit7/context-skills](https://github.com/bradspit7/context-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/analyze-handoff))
- **analyze-har**: Analyze a HAR file (HTTP Archive — exported from Chrome/Firefox DevTools → Network tab → Save all as HAR with content) and produce a normalized inventory of the endpoints it captured, with inferred request/response schemas, auth scheme detection, and a human-readable summary. The zero-install capture path. Use when the user supplies a .har file, says "analyze this HAR", "what endpoints does this site call", or "I exported the network tab, now what". ([danielrosehill/Claude-Browser-Data-Capture-Plugin](https://github.com/danielrosehill/Claude-Browser-Data-Capture-Plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/analyze-har) · ★1)
- **analyze-harvest-loss**: Analyze harvest loss from supplied records, preserving original observations, units, uncertainty, and conflicting evidence. Use for analyze harvest loss; review assistance only. ([jeremylongworth-source/AgentCannabis](https://github.com/jeremylongworth-source/AgentCannabis/tree/HEAD/skills/analyze-harvest-loss) · ★2)
- **analyze-heap**: Analyses a heap dump held by a running Jeffrey Microscope — what is holding the memory, what is leaking, which class loader survived a redeploy, where the waste is — starting from the catalogue or from a .hprof file Jeffrey has not seen yet. Use whenever the user asks what is holding memory, why the heap keeps growing, why the JVM ran out of memory, what is leaking, or mentions retained size, a dominator tree, GC roots, a heap dump or an .hprof file. ([petrbouda/jeffrey](https://github.com/petrbouda/jeffrey/tree/HEAD/jeffrey-agent-plugin/skills/analyze-heap) · ★81)
- **analyze-hearing**: Анализ транскрипции судебного заседания. Используй, когда юрист говорит «разбери транскрипцию», «проанализируй заседание», «что сказал судья», «что говорил оппонент», «найди речевые паттерны», «составь выводы по заседанию». Скилл принимает текстовую транскрипцию, сохраняет её в каталог заседания и делегирует Opus-subagent аналитическую работу по контракту 3.1c. Итоговый артефакт — markdown-файл `hearings/YYYY-MM-DD/analysis.md` без `.docx`. ([strigov/vassal-litigator](https://github.com/strigov/vassal-litigator/tree/HEAD/skills/analyze-hearing) · ★8)
- **analyze-helm-k8s**: Derive effective runtime config from Helm charts or K8s manifests; produce runtime-config.md with file:line citations. ([eldarush/qaas-copilot](https://github.com/eldarush/qaas-copilot/tree/HEAD/plugins/qaas/skills/analyze-helm-k8s))
- **analyze-heterogeneity**: Identify clinical, methodological, and statistical heterogeneity sources and prioritize subgroup/meta-regression/outlier investigation. ([yogsoth-ai/de-anthropocentric-research-engine](https://github.com/yogsoth-ai/de-anthropocentric-research-engine/tree/HEAD/skills/analyze-heterogeneity) · ★512)
- **analyze-heterogeneous-effects**: Use when a trial or evaluation has its main result and someone asks "who did it work for?" or "did it work better for women or poorer households?", or wants machine learning to find who responds. Also use when a team is about to report a subgroup difference it never planned to test. Tests the subgroups the analysis plan named, labels every other split a hypothesis, and says what to pre-register next. Do not use before the trial, to choose subgroups; use write-pre-analysis-plan. Not for whether an effect will hold at scale; use assess-scalability. ([agency-fund/besci-skills](https://github.com/agency-fund/besci-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/analyze-heterogeneous-effects) · ★3)
- **analyze-high-unemployment-high-gdp-growth-fiscal-deficit-scenari**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/data/analyze-high-unemployment-high-gdp-growth-fiscal-deficit-scenari) · ★745 · 2 copies)
- **analyze-high-unemployment-high-gdp-growth-fiscal-deficit-scenarios**: 在「失業率走高／勞動市場轉弱」但「名目或實質 GDP 仍維持高位（或仍在成長）」的情境下，依據歷史關聯估算美國財政赤字占 GDP（Deficit/GDP）可能擴張的區間，並生成對長天期美債（長久期 UST）供給/利率風險的情境解讀。支援視覺化圖表輸出。 ([diegosouzapw/awesome-omni-skill](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/awesome-omni-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/devops/analyze-high-unemployment-high-gdp-growth-fiscal-deficit-scenarios) · ★67 · 4 copies)
- **analyze-historic-place-vitality**: 收到历史文化街区、历史城区、传统街巷、步行街、文化遗产地、历史建筑群或相关地点后立即执行完整公开网络活力研究：检索不少于150个去重相关网页，以不少于100个有效评分样本为目标并在不足时自动开展深度迭代检索，使用可审计的语义方法把缺少原生数值的文字编码为九维主题、倾向、强度和置信度，完成九维用户感知分析，生成详细可追溯的DOCX报告和含摘要原文及语义轨迹的七表XLSX工作簿。用于用户要求联网分析评价、游记、社交内容、地图信息、新闻、论文、专业或官方资料并保留完整来源链时；每次研究只使用本次任务重新取得的证据，不使用模型记忆、旧对话或旧报告内容，不绕过登录、验证码或反爬限制。 ([Right-068/analyze-historic-place-vitality](https://github.com/Right-068/analyze-historic-place-vitality/tree/HEAD/versions/v1.3.0))
